1	// 多人联机共享协议层（客户端与 Node 服务器共用，docs/multiplayer-design.md §1-2）。
2	// 帧格式对齐原版：[u16 len][u8 msgId][payload...]，小端，包上限 65535B。
3	// 消息 ID 尽量沿用原版 MessageID 编号（Hello=1/SLOT=3/WorldData=7/TileSection=10/
4	// PlayerSpawn=12/PlayerControls=13/TileManipulation=17/SetTime=18/NetModules=82/Ping=154）；
5	// 编码字段为本仓库 v1 简化集（protoVer 门禁，protoVer=1）。
6	
7	export const PROTO_MAGIC = 'SW1';
8	export const PROTO_VER = 1;
9	
10	/** 消息 ID（v1 实现范围；编号对齐原版 MessageID.cs） */
11	export const enum Msg {
12	  Hello = 1,          // C→S {magic, protoVer, name}
13	  Kick = 2,           // S→C {reason}
14	  PlayerSlot = 3,     // S→C {slot, seed 随机}
15	  SyncPlayer = 4,     // 双向 {slot, appearanceJson}
16	  RequestWorldData = 6, // C→S {}
17	  WorldData = 7,      // S→C {时间/尺寸/出生点/层线/flags/seed/name}
18	  SpawnTileData = 8,  // C→S {x, y}（客户端请求出生点周围 section）
19	  StatusText = 9,     // S→C {count}（将发的 strip 数，进度条）
20	  TileSection = 10,   // S→C {x0,y0,w,h, rleBytes}（200×20 条带）
21	  PlayerSpawn = 12,   // S→C {slot, x, y}（进房落点确认）
22	  PlayerState = 13,   // C→S→广播 {slot,x,y,vx,vy,facing,sel,dead}
23	  PlayerActive = 14,  // S→C 广播 {slot, active, name}
24	  TileBatch = 17,     // C→S→广播 {count, ops[]}（tile 操作批量，对齐 msg17 语义）
25	  SetTime = 18,       // S→C {timeOfDay, dayCount}
26	  NetModules = 82,    // 双向 {moduleId, ...}（module1=聊天 module2=ping）
27	  Ping = 154,         // 简化独立心跳（module2 并存预留）
28	}
29	
30	/** NetModule 表（对齐原版注册顺序，显式建表防错位） */
31	export const enum NetModule {
32	  Liquid = 0,   // 预留（v1 液体客户端本地）
33	  Text = 1,     // 聊天 {authorSlot, text, r, g, b}
34	  PingModule = 2, // 预留
35	}
36	
37	// ================= Writer（小端，定宽；字符串 = u16 长度 + UTF-8） =================
38	
39	export class NetWriter {
40	  private buf = new Uint8Array(256);
41	  private len = 0;
42	  private dv: DataView;
43	
44	  constructor(private readonly msgId: number) {
45	    // 预留 [u16 len][u8 msgId]
46	    this.len = 3;
47	    this.dv = new DataView(this.buf.buffer);
48	  }
49	
50	  private grow(need: number) {
51	    if (this.len + need <= this.buf.length) return;
52	    let cap = this.buf.length;
53	    while (cap < this.len + need) cap *= 2;
54	    const nb = new Uint8Array(cap);
55	    nb.set(this.buf.subarray(0, this.len));
56	    this.buf = nb;
57	    this.dv = new DataView(this.buf.buffer);
58	  }
59	
60	  u8(v: number): this { this.grow(1); this.buf[this.len++] = v & 255; return this; }
61	  i8(v: number): this { return this.u8(v < 0 ? v + 256 : v); }
62	  u16(v: number): this { this.grow(2); this.dv.setUint16(this.len, v, true); this.len += 2; return this; }
63	  i16(v: number): this { this.grow(2); this.dv.setInt16(this.len, v, true); this.len += 2; return this; }
64	  u32(v: number): this { this.grow(4); this.dv.setUint32(this.len, v >>> 0, true); this.len += 4; return this; }
65	  i32(v: number): this { this.grow(4); this.dv.setInt32(this.len, v, true); this.len += 4; return this; }
66	  f32(v: number): this { this.grow(4); this.dv.setFloat32(this.len, v, true); this.len += 4; return this; }
67	  f64(v: number): this { this.grow(8); this.dv.setFloat64(this.len, v, true); this.len += 8; return this; }
68	  bool(v: boolean): this { return this.u8(v ? 1 : 0); }
69	  bytes(b: Uint8Array): this { this.grow(b.length); this.buf.set(b, this.len); this.len += b.length; return this; }
70	  str(s: string): this {
71	    const b = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
72	    this.u16(b.length);
73	    return this.bytes(b);
74	  }
75	
76	  /** 回填长度前缀并返回完整帧（含 [u16 len][u8 id]） */
77	  finish(): Uint8Array {
78	    if (this.len - 3 > 65535) throw new Error(`net: 包超限 ${this.len}`);
79	    this.dv.setUint16(0, this.len, true);
80	    this.buf[2] = this.msgId & 255;
81	    return this.buf.subarray(0, this.len);
82	  }
83	}
84	
85	// ================= Reader =================
86	
87	export class NetReader {
88	  private dv: DataView;
89	  private p = 0;
90	  constructor(private readonly buf: Uint8Array) {
91	    this.dv = new DataView(buf.buffer, buf.byteOffset, buf.byteLength);
92	  }
93	  get pos() { return this.p; }
94	  u8(): number { return this.buf[this.p++]; }
95	  i8(): number { const v = this.buf[this.p++]; return v >= 128 ? v - 256 : v; }
96	  u16(): number { const v = this.dv.getUint16(this.p, true); this.p += 2; return v; }
97	  i16(): number { const v = this.dv.getInt16(this.p, true); this.p += 2; return v; }
98	  u32(): number { const v = this.dv.getUint32(this.p, true); this.p += 4; return v; }
99	  i32(): number { const v = this.dv.getInt32(this.p, true); this.p += 4; return v; }
100	  f32(): number { const v = this.dv.getFloat32(this.p, true); this.p += 4; return v; }
101	  f64(): number { const v = this.dv.getFloat64(this.p, true); this.p += 8; return v; }
102	  bool(): boolean { return this.u8() !== 0; }
103	  bytes(n: number): Uint8Array { const b = this.buf.subarray(this.p, this.p + n); this.p += n; return b; }
104	  str(): string {
105	    const n = this.u16();
106	    return new TextDecoder().decode(this.bytes(n));
107	  }
108	}
109	
110	// ================= 帧流解析（粘包；对齐原版 CheckBytes 语义） =================
111	
112	/** 字节流帧解析器：append 后逐帧回调 {msgId, payloadReader}，自动处理半包 */
113	export class FrameParser {
114	  private chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
115	  private total = 0;
116	
117	  append(data: Uint8Array) {
118	    // 拷贝（ws 回调数据可能被复用）
119	    this.chunks.push(new Uint8Array(data));
120	    this.total += data.length;
121	  }
122	
123	  /** 解析所有完整帧；返回 false 表示缓冲为空 */
124	  drain(cb: (msgId: number, r: NetReader) => void): boolean {
125	    if (this.total < 3) return this.total > 0;
126	    // 合并缓冲
127	    let buf = new Uint8Array(this.total);
128	    let off = 0;
129	    for (const c of this.chunks) { buf.set(c, off); off += c.length; }
130	    this.chunks = [];
131	    this.total = 0;
132	    let p = 0;
133	    let any = false;
134	    while (p + 3 <= buf.length) {
135	      const dv = new DataView(buf.buffer, buf.byteOffset, buf.byteLength);
136	      const len = dv.getUint16(p, true);
137	      if (len < 3 || len > 65535) { break; } // 脏流：丢弃
138	      if (p + len > buf.length) break; // 半包
139	      const msgId = buf[p + 2];
140	      cb(msgId, new NetReader(buf.subarray(p + 3, p + len)));
141	      p += len;
142	      any = true;
143	    }
144	    if (p < buf.length) { this.chunks.push(buf.subarray(p)); this.total = buf.length - p; }
145	    return any || this.total > 0;
146	  }
147	}
148	
149	// ================= tile 操作批量（msg17，对齐原版单点操作语义的批量变体） =================
150	
151	export const enum TileOpAction {
152	  SetTile = 0,    // v=type, fx, fy
153	  SetWall = 1,    // v=wall
154	  SetHalfBrick = 5,
155	  SetSlope = 6,
156	  SetActuated = 3,
157	  SetWire = 4,    // v=bits
158	}
159	
160	export interface TileOp {
161	  a: TileOpAction;
162	  x: number; y: number;
163	  v: number;
164	  fx: number; fy: number;
165	}
166	
167	export function writeTileBatch(ops: TileOp[]): Uint8Array {
168	  const w = new NetWriter(Msg.TileBatch);
169	  w.u16(ops.length);
170	  for (const o of ops) {
171	    w.u8(o.a);
172	    w.i32(o.x); w.i32(o.y);
173	    w.u16(o.v & 0xffff);
174	    if (o.a === TileOpAction.SetTile) { w.u16(o.fx); w.u16(o.fy); }
175	  }
176	  return w.finish();
177	}
178	
179	export function readTileBatch(r: NetReader): TileOp[] {
180	  const n = r.u16();
181	  const ops: TileOp[] = [];
182	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
183	    const a = r.u8() as TileOpAction;
184	    const x = r.i32(), y = r.i32(), v = r.u16();
185	    let fx = 0, fy = 0;
186	    if (a === TileOpAction.SetTile) { fx = r.u16(); fy = r.u16(); }
187	    ops.push({ a, x, y, v, fx, fy });
188	  }
189	  return ops;
190	}
191	
192	// ================= tile strip RLE 编解码（msg10，codecVer=0 裸 RLE） =================
193	// 每 tile 完整状态 = type/flags/frameX/frameY/wall/liquid/liquidType/half/slope/wire
194	// 11B 裸；RLE 按"全状态相同"连续段编码 [u16 count][11B 状态]，地下均质区压缩比极高。
195	// 条带默认 200×20（4400 格，最坏 57KB < 64KB 包上限）。
196	
197	export interface StripRect { x0: number; y0: number; w: number; h: number; }
198	
199	/** 编码一条 strip（读 store 指定矩形） */
200	export function encodeStrip(
201	  st: { w: number; h: number; type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array;
202	        wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array; liquidType: Uint8Array; half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array },
203	  rect: StripRect,
204	): Uint8Array {
205	  const w = new NetWriter(Msg.TileSection);
206	  w.i32(rect.x0); w.i32(rect.y0); w.u16(rect.w); w.u16(rect.h);
207	  const idx = (x: number, y: number) => y * st.w + x;
208	  let run = 0;
209	  let rType = 0, rFlags = 0, rFx = 0, rFy = 0, rWall = 0, rLiq = 0, rLt = 0, rHalf = 0, rSlope = 0, rWire = 0;
210	  const flushRun = () => {
211	    if (run === 0) return;
212	    w.u16(run);
213	    w.u16(rType); w.u8(rFlags); w.u16(rFx); w.u16(rFy); w.u16(rWall);
214	    w.u8(rLiq); w.u8(rLt); w.u8(rHalf); w.u8(rSlope); w.u8(rWire);
215	    run = 0;
216	  };
217	  for (let y = rect.y0; y < rect.y0 + rect.h; y++) {
218	    for (let x = rect.x0; x < rect.x0 + rect.w; x++) {
219	      const i = idx(x, y);
220	      if (run > 0 && (
221	        st.type[i] !== rType || st.flags[i] !== rFlags || st.frameX[i] !== rFx || st.frameY[i] !== rFy
222	        || st.wall[i] !== rWall || st.liquid[i] !== rLiq || st.liquidType[i] !== rLt
223	        || st.half[i] !== rHalf || st.slope[i] !== rSlope || st.wire[i] !== rWire
224	      )) flushRun();
225	      if (run === 0) {
226	        rType = st.type[i]; rFlags = st.flags[i]; rFx = st.frameX[i]; rFy = st.frameY[i];
227	        rWall = st.wall[i]; rLiq = st.liquid[i]; rLt = st.liquidType[i];
228	        rHalf = st.half[i]; rSlope = st.slope[i]; rWire = st.wire[i];
229	      }
230	      run++;
231	    }
232	  }
233	  flushRun();
234	  return w.finish();
235	}
236	
237	/** 解码一条 strip 直写目标 store（批量路径：绕过事件，调用方负责事后整块标脏） */
238	export function decodeStrip(
239	  st: { w: number; h: number; type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array;
240	        wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array; liquidType: Uint8Array; half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array },
241	  r: NetReader,
242	): StripRect | null {
243	  const x0 = r.i32(), y0 = r.i32(), w = r.u16(), h = r.u16();
244	  if (x0 < 0 || y0 < 0 || x0 + w > st.w || y0 + h > st.h) return null;
245	  let written = 0;
246	  while (written < w * h) {
247	    const count = r.u16();
248	    const type = r.u16(), flags = r.u8(), fx = r.u16(), fy = r.u16(), wall = r.u16();
249	    const liq = r.u8(), lt = r.u8(), half = r.u8(), slope = r.u8(), wire = r.u8();
250	    for (let k = 0; k < count && written < w * h; k++) {
251	      const x = x0 + (written % w), y = y0 + Math.floor(written / w);
252	      const i = y * st.w + x;
253	      st.type[i] = type; st.flags[i] = flags; st.frameX[i] = fx; st.frameY[i] = fy; st.wall[i] = wall;
254	      st.liquid[i] = liq; st.liquidType[i] = lt; st.half[i] = half; st.slope[i] = slope; st.wire[i] = wire;
255	      written++;
256	    }
257	  }
258	  return { x0, y0, w, h };
259	}
260	

```

34	
35	async function loadWorld(): Promise<World> {

51	
52	const world = await loadWorld();

55	// 自动存档

73	
74	// ================= 客户端（slot 模型对齐原版 RemoteClient） =================
75	
76	const MAX_PLAYERS = 255;
77	
78	interface Client {
79	  ws: WebSocket;
80	  parser: FrameParser;
81	  slot: number;           // -1 = 未分配
82	  state: number;          // 对齐原版：0 连接 / 1 过握手 / 10 在游戏
83	  name: string;
84	  appearance: string;     // appearance JSON
85	  lastSeen: number;       // tick 计数（超时踢）
86	  /** 已下发的条带集合（"section 兴趣管理"的条带粒度变体） */
87	  sentStrips: Set<string>;
88	}
89	
90	const clients = new Set<Client>();
91	const slotUsed = new Array<boolean>(MAX_PLAYERS).fill(false);
92	
93	function allocSlot(): number {
94	  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYERS; i++) if (!slotUsed[i]) { slotUsed[i] = true; return i; }
95	  return -1;
96	}
97	
98	function send(c: Client, frame: Uint8Array) {
99	  if (c.ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) c.ws.send(frame);
100	}
101	
102	function broadcast(frame: Uint8Array, except?: Client) {
103	  for (const c of clients) {
104	    if (c === except || c.state < 10) continue;
105	    send(c, frame);
106	  }
107	}
108	
109	// ================= section 流式下发（对齐原版 SpawnTileData → 5×3 section 语义，条带粒度） =================
110	
111	const STRIP_W = 200;
112	const STRIP_H = 20;
113	
114	function stripsAround(cx: number, cy: number): Array<{ x0: number; y0: number }> {
115	  const out: Array<{ x0: number; y0: number }> = [];
116	  const sx = Math.floor(cx / STRIP_W), sy = Math.floor(cy / STRIP_H);
117	  for (let dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++) {
118	    for (let dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
119	      const x0 = (sx + dx) * STRIP_W, y0 = (sy + dy) * STRIP_H;
120	      if (x0 >= 0 && y0 >= 0 && x0 < st.w && y0 < st.h) out.push({ x0, y0 });
121	    }
122	  }
123	  return out;
124	}
125	
126	function sendWorldStrips(c: Client, cx: number, cy: number) {
127	  const strips = stripsAround(cx, cy);
128	  const fresh = strips.filter((s) => !c.sentStrips.has(`${s.x0},${s.y0}`));
129	  send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.StatusText).u16(fresh.length).finish());
130	  for (const s of fresh) {
131	    c.sentStrips.add(`${s.x0},${s.y0}`);
132	    const frame = encodeStrip(st, { x0: s.x0, y0: s.y0, w: Math.min(STRIP_W, st.w - s.x0), h: Math.min(STRIP_H, st.h - s.y0) });
133	    send(c, frame);
134	  }
135	}
136	
137	// ================= WorldData（msg7） =================
138	
139	function worldDataFrame(): Uint8Array {
140	  const w = new NetWriter(Msg.WorldData);
141	  w.f64(world.clock.timeOfDay);
142	  w.u32(world.clock.dayCount);
143	  w.u16(st.w); w.u16(st.h);
144	  w.i32(world.spawnX); w.i32(world.spawnY);
145	  w.f32(world.groundLevel); w.f32(world.rockLevel); w.f32(world.lavaLine);
146	  w.i32(world.seed);
147	  w.str(world.name);
148	  w.bool(world.crimson);
149	  w.i32(world.dungeonX); w.i32(world.dungeonY); w.i32(world.jungleX);
150	  const keys = Object.keys(world.flags);
151	  w.u16(keys.length);
152	  for (const k of keys) { w.str(k); w.bool(!!world.flags[k]); }
153	  return w.finish();
154	}
155	
156	// ================= 时间同步 =================
157	
158	setInterval(() => {
159	  const f = new NetWriter(Msg.SetTime).f64(world.clock.timeOfDay).u32(world.clock.dayCount).finish();
160	  for (const c of clients) if (c.state >= 10) send(c, f);
161	}, 10000);
162	// 服务器侧时钟推进（对齐原版服务器跑 WorldGen.UpdateWorld 时钟）
163	setInterval(() => {
164	  world.clock.tick(1000);
165	}, 1000);
166	
167	// ================= tile 操作应用（服务器权威世界，对齐原版 msg17→WorldGen→广播） =================
168	
169	function applyTileOps(ops: TileOp[]) {
170	  for (const o of ops) {
171	    if (o.x < 0 || o.y < 0 || o.x >= st.w || o.y >= st.h) continue;
172	    switch (o.a) {
173	      case TileOpAction.SetTile: st.setTileSilent(o.x, o.y, o.v, o.fx, o.fy); break;
174	      case TileOpAction.SetWall: st.setWallSilent(o.x, o.y, o.v); break;
175	      case TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick: st.half[st.idx(o.x, o.y)] = o.v ? 1 : 0; break;
176	      case TileOpAction.SetSlope: st.slope[st.idx(o.x, o.y)] = o.v; break;
177	      case TileOpAction.SetActuated: st.setActuated(o.x, o.y, !!o.v); break;
178	      case TileOpAction.SetWire: st.setWireSilent(o.x, o.y, o.v); break;
179	    }
180	  }
181	}
182	
183	// ================= 消息分发（对齐原版 MessageBuffer.GetData 的 switch） =================
184	
185	function handle(c: Client, msgId: number, r: NetReader) {
186	  c.lastSeen = 0;
187	  switch (msgId) {
188	    case Msg.Hello: {
189	      const magic = r.str();
190	      const ver = r.u16();
191	      c.name = r.str();
192	      if (magic !== PROTO_MAGIC || ver !== PROTO_VER) {
193	        send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.Kick).str(`协议不匹配（期望 ${PROTO_MAGIC} v${PROTO_VER}）`).finish());
194	        c.ws.close();
195	        return;
196	      }
197	      const slot = allocSlot();
198	      if (slot < 0) { send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.Kick).str('服务器已满').finish()); c.ws.close(); return; }
199	      c.slot = slot;
200	      c.state = 1;
201	      send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerSlot).u8(slot).finish());
202	      console.log(`[net] slot ${slot} 握手通过 (${c.name})`);
203	      return;
204	    }
205	    case Msg.RequestWorldData: {
206	      if (c.slot < 0) return;
207	      c.state = 2;
208	      send(c, worldDataFrame());
209	      return;
210	    }
211	    case Msg.SpawnTileData: {
212	      if (c.slot < 0) return;
213	      const x = r.i32(), y = r.i32();
214	      sendWorldStrips(c, x, y);
215	      send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerSpawn).u8(c.slot).i32(world.spawnX).i32(world.spawnY).finish());
216	      // 向已在场的玩家宣告新玩家，并向新玩家同步在场玩家
217	      broadcast(new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerActive).u8(c.slot).bool(true).str(c.name).finish());
218	      send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.SyncPlayer).u8(c.slot).str(c.appearance).finish());
219	      for (const other of clients) {
220	        if (other === c || other.state < 10) continue;
221	        send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerActive).u8(other.slot).bool(true).str(other.name).finish());
222	        send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.SyncPlayer).u8(other.slot).str(other.appearance).finish());
223	      }
224	      c.state = 10;
225	      console.log(`[net] slot ${c.slot} 进入世界，在线 ${onlineCount()}`);
226	      return;
227	    }
228	    case Msg.SyncPlayer: {
229	      // 外观（进房后到达也接受；广播给其他人）
230	      c.appearance = r.str();
231	      broadcast(new NetWriter(Msg.SyncPlayer).u8(c.slot).str(c.appearance).finish(), c);
232	      return;
233	    }
234	    case Msg.PlayerState: {
235	      if (c.state < 10) return;
236	      // 服务器覆写 slot 防冒用（对齐原版 index=whoAmI）
237	      const f = new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerState);
238	      f.u8(c.slot);
239	      f.f32(r.f32()); f.f32(r.f32());  // x, y
240	      f.f32(r.f32()); f.f32(r.f32());  // vx, vy
241	      f.i8(r.i8());                     // facing
242	      f.u8(r.u8());                     // selectedItem
243	      f.bool(r.bool());                 // dead
244	      broadcast(f.finish(), c);
245	      return;
246	    }
247	    case Msg.TileBatch: {
248	      if (c.state < 10) return;
249	      const ops = readTileBatch(r);
250	      applyTileOps(ops);
251	      broadcast(writeTileBatch(ops), c); // 原样中继（原版语义：服务器执行后广播）
252	      return;
253	    }
254	    case Msg.NetModules: {
255	      const moduleId = r.u16();
256	      if (moduleId === NetModule.Text) {
257	        // 聊天：服务器回填作者 slot 广播（对齐 NetTextModule.SerializeServerMessage）
258	        const text = r.str();
259	        if (!text || text.length > 200) return;
260	        const f = new NetWriter(Msg.NetModules);
261	        f.u16(NetModule.Text);
262	        f.u8(c.slot);
263	        f.str(text);
264	        f.u8(255); f.u8(220); f.u8(120);
265	        broadcast(f.finish());
266	        console.log(`[chat] <slot${c.slot} ${c.name}> ${text}`);
267	      }
268	      return;
269	    }
270	    case Msg.Ping: {
271	      const ts = r.f64();
272	      send(c, new NetWriter(Msg.Ping).f64(ts).finish());
273	      return;
274	    }
275	    default:
276	      return; // 未知消息跳过（对齐原版 b >= Count 丢弃）
277	  }
278	}
279	
280	function onlineCount() {
281	  let n = 0;
282	  for (const c of clients) if (c.state >= 10) n++;
283	  return n;
284	}
285	
286	// ================= WebSocket 服务器 =================
287	
288	const wss = new WebSocketServer({ port: PORT });
289	wss.on('connection', (ws) => {
290	  ws.binaryType = 'nodebuffer';
291	  const c: Client = { ws, parser: new FrameParser(), slot: -1, state: 0, name: '?', appearance: '{}', lastSeen: 0, sentStrips: new Set() };
292	  clients.add(c);
293	  ws.on('message', (data: Buffer) => {
294	    c.parser.append(new Uint8Array(data));
295	    c.parser.drain((msgId, r) => handle(c, msgId, r));
296	  });
297	  ws.on('close', () => {
298	    clients.delete(c);
299	    if (c.slot >= 0) {
300	      slotUsed[c.slot] = false;
301	      broadcast(new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerActive).u8(c.slot).bool(false).str('').finish());
302	    }
303	    console.log(`[net] slot ${c.slot} 断开，在线 ${onlineCount()}`);
304	  });
305	  ws.on('error', () => { /* close 会跟上 */ });
306	});
307	
308	// 超时看门狗（对齐原版 TimeOutTimer 7200t=120s 语义，1s 粒度）
309	setInterval(() => {
310	  for (const c of clients) {
311	    c.lastSeen += 1;
312	    if (c.lastSeen > 120) {
313	      console.log(`[net] slot ${c.slot} 超时踢出`);
314	      c.ws.close();
315	    }
316	  }
317	}, 1000);
318	
319	// ================= LAN 广播（对齐原版 Netplay.BroadcastThread UDP:8888） =================
320	
321	const LAN_MAGIC = 1010;
322	const lan = dgram.createSocket({ type: 'udp4' });
323	const broadcastAddr = (() => {
324	  const os = node_os;
325	  for (const list of Object.values(os.networkInterfaces())) {
326	    for (const ni of list ?? []) {
327	      if (ni.family === 'IPv4' && !ni.internal) {
328	        // 末段置 255
329	        return ni.address.split('.').slice(0, 3).join('.') + '.255';
330	      }
331	    }
332	  }
333	  return '255.255.255.255';
334	})();
335	lan.bind(() => {
336	  lan.setBroadcast(true);
337	  setInterval(() => {
338	    const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
339	      magic: LAN_MAGIC, port: PORT, name: world.name,
340	      host: 'host', w: st.w, h: st.h, crimson: world.crimson,
341	      online: onlineCount(), max: MAX_PLAYERS, hardmode: !!world.flags.hardMode,
342	    }));
343	    lan.send(payload, 8888, broadcastAddr);
344	  }, 1000);
345	});
346	
347	// LAN 发现 HTTP（浏览器无 UDP，同网段可探测本机；CORS 开放）
348	http.createServer((req, res) => {
349	  res.writeHead(200, {
350	    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
351	    'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
352	  });
353	  res.end(JSON.stringify({
354	    ok: true, magic: LAN_MAGIC, port: PORT, name: world.name,
355	    w: st.w, h: st.h, online: onlineCount(), max: MAX_PLAYERS, hardmode: !!world.flags.hardMode,
356	  }));
357	}).listen(PORT + 1);
358	
359	console.log(`[server] ws://0.0.0.0:${PORT} （lan 发现 :${PORT + 1}/lan）世界 ${world.name} ${st.w}×${st.h}`);
360	

```

1	// 多人联机客户端（netMode==1，docs/multiplayer-design.md §4）。
2	// 握手 → 世界流式组装 → 玩家互见 + tile 操作上报/应用 + 时间对齐。
3	// v1 未同步（客户端本地）：NPC/弹幕/掉落/箱子/液体模拟结果。
4	import {
5	  PROTO_MAGIC, PROTO_VER, Msg, NetModule,
6	  NetWriter, NetReader, FrameParser, TileOp, TileOpAction, readTileBatch, decodeStrip,
7	} from './protocol';
8	import type { World } from '../world/World';
9	import { World as WorldCtor } from '../world/World';
10	import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
11	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
12	
13	/** 远端玩家状态（渲染用；Player 实例由 Game 持有池，这里只存同步数据） */
14	export interface RemotePlayerState {
15	  slot: number;
16	  name: string;
17	  appearance: string;
18	  x: number; y: number; vx: number; vy: number;
19	  facing: number; selectedItem: number; dead: boolean;
20	  active: boolean;
21	}
22	
23	export interface ClientNetHooks {
24	  /** 世界组装完成（全部初始 strip 到齐 + PlayerSpawn）——Game 进 loadWorld */
25	  onWorldReady: (world: World) => void;
26	  /** 进度（label, p 0..1） */
27	  onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void;
28	  /** 聊天 */
29	  onChat?: (text: string, r: number, g: number, b: number) => void;
30	  /** 被踢 */
31	  onKick?: (reason: string) => void;
32	}
33	
34	export class ClientNet {
35	  active = false;
36	  mySlot = -1;
37	  players = new Map<number, RemotePlayerState>();
38	
39	  private ws: WebSocket | null = null;
40	  private parser = new FrameParser();
41	  private hooks: ClientNetHooks;
42	  private game: { player: { appearance?: unknown; inv: { slots: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> } } };
43	
44	  /** 组装中的世界（收到 msg7 建骨架，strip 到齐后交给 onWorldReady） */
45	  private pendingWorld: World | null = null;
46	  private pendingStrips = 0;
47	  private worldDelivered = false;
48	  /** 本地 tile 变更上报队列（TileStore.netReporter 收集） */
49	  private tileQueue: TileOp[] = [];
50	  private lastStateSent = 0;
51	  private lastSentPos = { x: 0, y: 0 };
52	
53	  constructor(
54	    game: ClientNet['game'],
55	    hooks: ClientNetHooks,
56	  ) {
57	    this.game = game;
58	    this.hooks = hooks;
59	  }
60	
61	  connect(url: string) {
62	    this.active = true;
63	    const ws = new WebSocket(url);
64	    ws.binaryType = 'arraybuffer';
65	    this.ws = ws;
66	    ws.onopen = () => {
67	      // Hello（对齐原版 msg1：版本校验）。连接时 Game.player 可能尚未创建（joinNetGame
68	      // 先连后 loadWorld）——外观名走可选链兜底
69	      const p = this.game.player as { appearance?: { name?: string } } | undefined | null;
70	      const name = p?.appearance?.name ?? '玩家';
71	      this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.Hello).str(PROTO_MAGIC).u16(PROTO_VER).str(name).finish());
72	    };
73	    ws.onmessage = (e) => {
74	      this.parser.append(new Uint8Array(e.data as ArrayBuffer));
75	      this.parser.drain((id, r) => this.handle(id, r));
76	    };
77	    ws.onclose = () => {
78	      if (this.active) {
79	        this.active = false;
80	        this.hooks.onKick?.('与服务器断开连接');
81	      }
82	    };
83	    ws.onerror = () => { /* close 跟上 */ };
84	  }
85	
86	  private send(frame: Uint8Array) {
87	    if (this.ws?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) this.ws.send(frame);
88	  }
89	
90	  disconnect() {
91	    this.active = false;
92	    this.ws?.close();
93	    this.ws = null;
94	  }
95	
96	  // ================= 收包分发（对齐原版 MessageBuffer switch） =================
97	
98	  private handle(msgId: number, r: NetReader) {
99	    switch (msgId) {
100	      case Msg.Kick: {
101	        this.hooks.onKick?.(r.str());
102	        this.disconnect();
103	        return;
104	      }
105	      case Msg.PlayerSlot: {
106	        this.mySlot = r.u8();
107	        // 全量上传自身（对齐原版 msg3 后立刻 SyncPlayer + RequestWorldData）
108	        const p2 = this.game.player as { appearance?: unknown } | undefined | null;
109	        const app = JSON.stringify(p2?.appearance ?? {});
110	        this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.SyncPlayer).u8(this.mySlot).str(app).finish());
111	        this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.RequestWorldData).finish());
112	        return;
113	      }
114	      case Msg.WorldData: {
115	        this.pendingWorld = this.readWorldData(r);
116	        return;
117	      }
118	      case Msg.StatusText: {
119	        this.pendingStrips = r.u16();
120	        this.hooks.onProgress?.('接收世界数据', 0);
121	        return;
122	      }
123	      case Msg.TileSection: {
124	        if (!this.pendingWorld) return;
125	        decodeStrip(this.pendingWorld.store, r);
126	        if (this.pendingStrips > 0) {
127	          this.pendingStrips--;
128	          this.hooks.onProgress?.('接收世界数据', 0.5);
129	        }
130	        return;
131	      }
132	      case Msg.PlayerSpawn: {
133	        const slot = r.u8();
134	        const sx = r.i32(), sy = r.i32();
135	        if (slot === this.mySlot && !this.worldDelivered && this.pendingWorld) {
136	          this.worldDelivered = true;
137	          this.pendingWorld.spawnX = sx;
138	          this.pendingWorld.spawnY = sy;
139	          this.hooks.onProgress?.('完成', 1);
140	          this.hooks.onWorldReady(this.pendingWorld);
141	          this.pendingWorld = null;
142	        }
143	        return;
144	      }
145	      case Msg.PlayerActive: {
146	        const slot = r.u8();
147	        const active = r.bool();
148	        const name = r.str();
149	        let p = this.players.get(slot);
150	        if (active) {
151	          if (!p) {
152	            p = { slot, name, appearance: '{}', x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, facing: 1, selectedItem: 0, dead: false, active: true };
153	            this.players.set(slot, p);
154	          }
155	          p.active = true;
156	          p.name = name || p.name;
157	        } else if (p) {
158	          p.active = false;
159	        }
160	        return;
161	      }
162	      case Msg.SyncPlayer: {
163	        const slot = r.u8();
164	        const appearance = r.str();
165	        const p = this.players.get(slot);
166	        if (p) p.appearance = appearance;
167	        return;
168	      }
169	      case Msg.PlayerState: {
170	        const slot = r.u8();
171	        let p = this.players.get(slot);
172	        if (!p) {
173	          p = { slot, name: `玩家${slot}`, appearance: '{}', x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, facing: 1, selectedItem: 0, dead: false, active: true };
174	          this.players.set(slot, p);
175	        }
176	        p.x = r.f32(); p.y = r.f32();
177	        p.vx = r.f32(); p.vy = r.f32();
178	        p.facing = r.i8();
179	        p.selectedItem = r.u8();
180	        p.dead = r.bool();
181	        return;
182	      }
183	      case Msg.TileBatch: {
184	        // 服务器中继的远端操作：应用 + 回环抑制
185	        const ops = readTileBatch(r);
186	        this.applyRemote(ops);
187	        return;
188	      }
189	      case Msg.SetTime: {
190	        // 时间对齐（服务器权威 clock）
191	        if (this.gameWorld) {
192	          const t = r.f64();
193	          const d = r.u32();
194	          if (Math.abs(this.gameWorld.clock.timeOfDay - t) > 0.005) {
195	            this.gameWorld.clock.timeOfDay = t;
196	          }
197	          this.gameWorld.clock.dayCount = d;
198	        }
199	        return;
200	      }
201	      case Msg.NetModules: {
202	        const moduleId = r.u16();
203	        if (moduleId === NetModule.Text) {
204	          const slot = r.u8();
205	          const text = r.str();
206	          const cr = r.u8(), cg = r.u8(), cb = r.u8();
207	          const name = this.players.get(slot)?.name ?? `玩家${slot}`;
208	          this.hooks.onChat?.(`<${name}> ${text}`, cr, cg, cb);
209	        }
210	        return;
211	      }
212	      case Msg.Ping:
213	        return; // 忽略回显
214	      default:
215	        return; // 未知跳过（对齐原版）
216	    }
217	  }
218	
219	  /** Game 侧设置运行期世界引用（时间对齐用） */
220	  gameWorld: World | null = null;
221	
222	  private readWorldData(r: NetReader): World {
223	    const time = r.f64();
224	    const dayCount = r.u32();
225	    const w = r.u16(), h = r.u16();
226	    const spawnX = r.i32(), spawnY = r.i32();
227	    const groundLevel = r.f32(), rockLevel = r.f32(), lavaLine = r.f32();
228	    const seed = r.i32();
229	    const name = r.str();
230	    const crimson = r.bool();
231	    const dungeonX = r.i32(), dungeonY = r.i32(), jungleX = r.i32();
232	    const flagCount = r.u16();
233	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
234	    for (let i = 0; i < flagCount; i++) flags[r.str()] = r.bool();
235	    const world = new WorldCtor(w, h, seed, name);
236	    world.clock.timeOfDay = time;
237	    world.clock.dayCount = dayCount;
238	    world.spawnX = spawnX; world.spawnY = spawnY;
239	    world.groundLevel = groundLevel; world.rockLevel = rockLevel; world.lavaLine = lavaLine;
240	    world.crimson = crimson;
241	    world.dungeonX = dungeonX; world.dungeonY = dungeonY; world.jungleX = jungleX;
242	    Object.assign(world.flags, flags);
243	    // 请求出生点周围 section（对齐原版 msg8）
244	    this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.SpawnTileData).i32(spawnX).i32(spawnY).finish());
245	    return world;
246	  }
247	
248	  // ================= tile 上报（TileStore.netReporter → 队列 → 每 tick 冲洗） =================
249	
250	  /** TileStore.netReporter 注入点；netSuppress 期间（应用远端操作）不收集 */
251	  reportTileOp(op: TileOp) {
252	    if (!this.active) return;
253	    if (this.tileQueue.length >= 256) return; // 防爆
254	    this.tileQueue.push(op);
255	  }
256	
257	  /** Game 每 fixedUpdate 调用：冲洗 tile 队列 + 玩家状态上报（变化驱动 + 66ms 节流） */
258	  tick() {
259	    if (!this.active) return;
260	    if (this.tileQueue.length) {
261	      // 每包最多 64 op（防超帧）
262	      const batch = this.tileQueue.splice(0, 64);
263	      const w = new NetWriter(Msg.TileBatch);
264	      w.u16(batch.length);
265	      for (const o of batch) {
266	        w.u8(o.a);
267	        w.i32(o.x); w.i32(o.y);
268	        w.u16(o.v & 0xffff);
269	        if (o.a === TileOpAction.SetTile) { w.u16(o.fx); w.u16(o.fy); }
270	      }
271	      this.send(w.finish());
272	    }
273	    this.sendPlayerState();
274	  }
275	
276	  private sendPlayerState() {
277	    const p = this.game.player as unknown as { cx: number; cy: number; vx: number; vy: number; facing: number; inv: { heldItem(): { id: number } | null }; dead: boolean };
278	    const now = performance.now();
279	    const moved = Math.abs(p.cx - this.lastSentPos.x) > 1 || Math.abs(p.cy - this.lastSentPos.y) > 1;
280	    if (!moved && now - this.lastStateSent < 1000) return; // 静止 1s 心跳
281	    if (now - this.lastStateSent < 66) return;             // 节流 ≥66ms（≈15Hz）
282	    this.lastStateSent = now;
283	    this.lastSentPos = { x: p.cx, y: p.cy };
284	    const held = p.inv.heldItem();
285	    this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.PlayerState)
286	      .f32(p.cx).f32(p.cy)
287	      .f32(p.vx).f32(p.vy)
288	      .i8(p.facing)
289	      .u8(held ? held.id : 0)
290	      .bool(p.dead).finish());
291	  }
292	
293	  // ================= 远端 tile 应用（回环抑制 + 事件链触发） =================
294	
295	  private applyRemote(ops: TileOp[]) {
296	    const st = this.gameWorld?.store;
297	    if (!st) return;
298	    st.netSuppress = true;
299	    for (const o of ops) {
300	      if (o.x < 0 || o.y < 0 || o.x >= st.w || o.y >= st.h) continue;
301	      switch (o.a) {
302	        case TileOpAction.SetTile: st.setTile(o.x, o.y, o.v, o.fx, o.fy); break;
303	        case TileOpAction.SetWall: st.setWall(o.x, o.y, o.v); break;
304	        case TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick: st.setHalfBrick(o.x, o.y, !!o.v); break;
305	        case TileOpAction.SetSlope: st.setSlope(o.x, o.y, o.v); break;
306	        case TileOpAction.SetActuated: st.setActuated(o.x, o.y, !!o.v); break;
307	        case TileOpAction.SetWire: st.setWire(o.x, o.y, o.v); break;
308	      }
309	    }
310	    st.netSuppress = false;
311	  }
312	
313	  /** 聊天发送 */
314	  sendChat(text: string) {
315	    this.send(new NetWriter(Msg.NetModules).u16(NetModule.Text).str(text).finish());
316	  }
317	}
318	

```

1	// SoA 多通道平面数组存储。所有热点代码只碰 TypedArray。
2	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
3	import { TileOpAction } from '../net/protocol';
4	
5	export class TileStore {
6	  readonly w: number;
7	  readonly h: number;
8	  readonly type: Uint16Array;
9	  readonly flags: Uint8Array; // bit0 active bit1 framedDecor占位(预留)
10	  readonly frameX: Uint16Array;
11	  readonly frameY: Uint16Array;
12	  readonly wall: Uint16Array; // 原版 wld 墙 id（0-366）
13	  readonly liquid: Uint8Array;   // 0-255 液量
14	  readonly liquidType: Uint8Array; // 0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜
15	  readonly half: Uint8Array;      // halfBrick 位（原版 Tile.cs bTileHeader bit1）：下半 8px 实心
16	  readonly slope: Uint8Array;     // 坡面 0-4（原版编码；渲染暂未支持，生成期少用）
17	  readonly wire: Uint8Array;      // 电路位（原版 Tile.cs sTileHeader）：bit0红 bit1蓝 bit2绿 bit3黄 bit4致动器 bit5已致动(inActive)
18	  // wire 位掩码常量（对应原版 Tile.cs:396-459 的 wire/wire2/wire3/wire4/actuator/inActive）
19	  static readonly WIRE_RED = 1;
20	  static readonly WIRE_BLUE = 2;
21	  static readonly WIRE_GREEN = 4;
22	  static readonly WIRE_YELLOW = 8;
23	  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATOR = 16;
24	  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATED = 32;
25	  static readonly WIRE_COLORS = 15; // 四色掩码
26	
27	  // tile 变更监听（chunk 缓存 / 光照脏标记 / 液体模拟统一从这里拿事件）
28	  private listeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
29	
30	  // ---- 多人联机上报钩子（ClientNet 注入；单机为 null 零开销） ----
31	  /** 本地 tile 变更上报（协议层 TileOp 收集器）。liquid 通道不报（客户端本地模拟） */
32	  netReporter: ((op: import('../net/protocol').TileOp) => void) | null = null;
33	  /** 应用远端操作期间置位：上报钩子静默（防回环） */
34	  netSuppress = false;
35	
36	  constructor(w: number, h: number, bufs?: import('../workers/protocol').TileBuffers) {
37	    this.w = w; this.h = h;
38	    // buffers 注入（worker transfer 重建路径）：直接以移交的 ArrayBuffer 包装 TypedArray，
39	    // 跳过分配与拷贝。缺省 explored 由调用方（World.fromPacket）补齐
40	    if (bufs) {
41	      this.type = new Uint16Array(bufs.type);
42	      this.flags = new Uint8Array(bufs.flags);
43	      this.frameX = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameX);
44	      this.frameY = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameY);
45	      this.wall = new Uint16Array(bufs.wall);
46	      this.liquid = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquid);
47	      this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquidType);
48	      this.half = new Uint8Array(bufs.half);
49	      this.slope = new Uint8Array(bufs.slope);
50	      this.wire = new Uint8Array(bufs.wire);
51	      return;
52	    }
53	    const n = w * h;
54	    this.type = new Uint16Array(n);
55	    this.flags = new Uint8Array(n);
56	    this.frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
57	    this.frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
58	    this.wall = new Uint16Array(n);
59	    this.liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
60	    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
61	    this.half = new Uint8Array(n);
62	    this.slope = new Uint8Array(n);
63	    this.wire = new Uint8Array(n);
64	  }
65	
66	  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }
67	
68	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
69	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
70	  }
71	
72	  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }
73	
74	  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
75	    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
76	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
77	      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
78	  }
79	
80	  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
81	  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
82	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
83	  }
84	
85	  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
86	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
87	    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
88	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
89	    return !!d && d.solid;
90	  }
91	
92	  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
93	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
94	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
95	    return !!d && d.platform;
96	  }
97	
98	  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
99	    this.listeners.push(fn);
100	  }
101	
102	  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
103	   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
104	  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
105	  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
106	    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
107	  }
108	
109	  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
110	  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
111	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
112	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
113	    this.type[i] = type;
114	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
115	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
116	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
117	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
118	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetTile, x, y, v: type, fx: frameX, fy: frameY });
119	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
120	  }
121	
122	  /** 运行期改半砖/坡面位（锤子交互用；生成期直接写数组即可） */
123	  setHalfBrick(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
124	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
125	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
126	    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
127	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
128	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
129	  }
130	
131	  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
132	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
133	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
134	    this.slope[i] = s;
135	    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
136	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetSlope, x, y, v: s, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
137	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
138	  }
139	
140	  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
141	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
142	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
143	    this.type[i] = type;
144	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
145	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
146	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
147	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
148	  }
149	
150	  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
151	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
152	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
153	    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
154	      this.wall[i] = wall;
155	      if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWall, x, y, v: wall, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
156	      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
157	    }
158	  }
159	
160	  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
161	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
162	    this.wall[this.idx(x, y)] = wall;
163	  }
164	
165	  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
166	   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
167	  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
168	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
169	    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
170	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
171	  }
172	
173	  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
174	    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
175	  }
176	
177	  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
178	  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
179	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
180	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
181	    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
182	    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
183	    this.wire[i] = b;
184	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
185	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
186	  }
187	
188	  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
189	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
190	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
191	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
192	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
193	    this.liquid[i] = a;
194	    this.liquidType[i] = type;
195	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
196	  }
197	}
198

4698	    const net = this.net!;
4699	    const list: Player[] = [];
4700	    for (const [slot, rp] of net.players) {
4701	      if (!rp.active || slot === net.mySlot) continue;
4702	      let proxy = this.remotePlayerProxies.find((p) => (p as unknown as { __netSlot?: number }).__netSlot === slot);
4703	      if (!proxy) {
4704	        proxy = new Player(rp.x, rp.y, this.player.inv); // 远程共享本地 inv 引用仅作渲染兜底（外观走 appearance）
4705	        (proxy as unknown as { __netSlot?: number }).__netSlot = slot;
4706	      }
4707	      proxy.x = rp.x; proxy.y = rp.y;
4708	      proxy.vx = rp.vx; proxy.vy = rp.vy;
4709	      proxy.facing = rp.facing;
4710	      proxy.dead = rp.dead;
4711	      proxy.animTime = (proxy.animTime + 1) % 1e9;
4712	      try { proxy.appearance = JSON.parse(rp.appearance); } catch { /* 保持默认 */ }
4713	      list.push(proxy);
4714	    }
4715	    this.remotePlayerProxies = list;
4716	  }
4717	}
4718	

```

144	  /** 玩家状态回填（worker/主线程两路共用） */
145	  function applyPlayer(g: Game, player: ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData>['player']) {
146	    g.player.hp = player.hp;
147	    g.player.x = player.x;
148	    g.player.y = player.y;
149	    // 上限扩容进度（水晶之心/生命果/魔力水晶；旧档缺省 100/20/20）
150	    if (player.baseMaxHp !== undefined) g.player.baseMaxHp = player.baseMaxHp;
151	    if (player.baseMaxMana !== undefined) g.player.baseMaxMana = player.baseMaxMana;
152	    if (player.mana !== undefined) g.player.mana = player.mana;
153	    g.player.inv.slots = player.inventory.concat(Array(Math.max(0, INV_SIZE - player.inventory.length)).fill(null));
154	    g.player.inv.selected = player.selected;
155	    // 玩家储物×4 回填（29/97/463/491；旧档缺省全空）
156	    if (player.banks) {
157	      for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) {
158	        const src = player.banks[b] ?? [];
159	        g.player.banks[b] = src.concat(Array(Math.max(0, 40 - src.length)).fill(null)).slice(0, 40);
160	      }
161	    }
162	  }
163	
164	  /** 按 IDB key 读档：主路径 worker 内直读 IDB（免大 JSON 字符串结构化克隆到
165	   *  worker 的主线程序列化开销——大存档实测秒级 100% CPU）；worker 不可用时
166	   *  才在主线程 kvGet 走 fallback */
167	  async function loadFromKey(key: string) {
168	    try {
169	      // worker 路径：IDB 读取 + JSON 解析 + RLE 解码 + load 模式沉降全在后台
170	      const { WorldGenClient } = await import('./workers/WorldGenClient');
171	      const client = new WorldGenClient();
172	      if (await client.probe()) {
173	        try {
174	          const { world, player } = await client.loadSave({ key }, {
175	            onProgress: (phase, p) => ui.showProgress(phase === 'settle' ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.SettleLiquids') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadingSave'), p),
176	          });
177	          const g = makeGame();

179	          applyPlayer(g, player as ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData>['player']);
180	          return;
181	        } catch (e) {
182	          if (!(e instanceof (await import('./workers/WorldGenClient')).WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e;
183	          // worker 失败 → 主线程 fallback
184	        }
185	      }
186	      const text = await kvGet(key);
187	      if (!text) { ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', '存档数据缺失')); return; }
188	      await loadFromJson(text);
189	    } catch (e) {
190	      console.error(e);
191	      ui.hideProgress();
192	      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', (e as Error).message));
193	    }
194	  }
195	
196	  async function loadFromJson(text: string) {
197	    try {
198	      // worker 路径：JSON 解析 + RLE 解码 + load 模式沉降全在后台（json 源传入）
199	      const { WorldGenClient } = await import('./workers/WorldGenClient');
200	      const client = new WorldGenClient();
201	      if (await client.probe()) {
202	        try {
203	          const { world, player } = await client.loadSave({ json: text }, {
204	            onProgress: (phase, p) => ui.showProgress(phase === 'settle' ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.SettleLiquids') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadingSave'), p),
205	          });
206	          const g = makeGame();
207	          await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => ui.showProgress(label, 0.8 + p * 0.2), { settled: true });
208	          applyPlayer(g, player as ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData>['player']);
209	          return;
210	        } catch (e) {
211	          if (!(e instanceof (await import('./workers/WorldGenClient')).WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e;
212	          // worker 失败 → 主线程 fallback
213	        }
214	      }
215	      const { world, player } = loadSave(text);
216	      const g = makeGame();
217	      ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadingSave'), 0.3);
218	      await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => ui.showProgress(label, 0.3 + p * 0.6));
219	      applyPlayer(g, player);
220	    } catch (e) {
221	      console.error(e);
222	      ui.hideProgress();
223	      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', (e as Error).message));
224	    }
225	  }

227	  async function importWld(buf: Uint8Array) {
228	    ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.ParsingWld'), 0.1);
229	    try {
230	      const { save, report, seedText, gameMode } = parseWldToSave(buf);
231	      (window as unknown as { __lastCompatReport?: unknown }).__lastCompatReport = report;
232	      ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.ConvertingWld'), 0.7);
233	      const g = makeGame();
234	      // 直接消费内存 SaveData(2026-08 审计 #3:此前的 stringify→parse 双拷贝
235	      // 每次导入多出 2×20~50MB 峰值,且让 5 份全图副本并存更久)
236	      const { world } = loadSaveData(save);
237	      await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => ui.showProgress(label, 0.7 + p * 0.3));
238	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY['copper_pickaxe'], 1);
239	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY['copper_axe'], 1);
240	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY['copper_sword'], 1);
241	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY['torch'], 20);
242	      ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.WldImported', save.header.name ?? '', save.header.wldVersion ?? 0));
243	      // 登记世界槽位并持久化：导入不再是一次性的，重进游戏可在世界列表中看到并继续游玩
244	      await worldStore.ensureLoaded();
245	      const names = new Set(worldStore.list().map((m) => m.name));
246	      let name = save.header.name;
247	      if (names.has(name)) {
248	        let i = 2;
249	        while (names.has(`${name} (${i})`)) i++;
250	        name = `${name} (${i})`;
251	      }
252	      currentWorld = await worldStore.register({
253	        name, seed: seedText || String(save.header.seed),
254	        w: save.header.width, h: save.header.height,
255	        difficulty: gameMode, evil: save.header.crimson ? 1 : 0,
256	      });
257	      doSave();
258	      // 兼容报告：有降级/跳过内容时弹窗
259	      const rpt = (window as unknown as { __lastCompatReport?: import('./ui/UI').CompatReport }).__lastCompatReport;
260	      if (rpt && (rpt.tilesDegraded.length || rpt.tilesCleared.length || rpt.itemsSkipped.length)) {
261	        ui.showCompatReport(rpt);
262	      }
263	    } catch (e) {
264	      console.error(e);
265	      ui.hideProgress();
266	      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.WldImportFailed', (e as Error).message));
267	    }
268	  }

352	        });
353	      },
354	      onBack: () => showCharacterSelect(),
355	    });
356	  }
357	
358	  /** 从世界槽位读取并进入游戏（worker 内直读 IDB：免大 JSON 字符串主线程读取
359	   *  + 结构化克隆双开销；fallback 时 worldStore.load 取回全文走 loadFromJson） */
360	  async function loadWorldFlow(meta: WorldMeta) {
361	    currentWorld = meta;
362	    await loadFromKey(`sandboxworld.world.${meta.id}`);
363	  }
364	
365	  /** 创建新世界：原版生成页（双进度条+实时地图预览）→ 注册槽位 → 进游戏 */
366	  async function createWorldFlow(cfg: { name: string; seed: string; w: number; h: number; difficulty: number; evil: -1 | 0 | 1 }) {
367	    const loadState = new UIWorldLoadState(cfg.evil);
368	    VUI.setState(loadState);
369	    const g = makeGame();
370	    await g.newWorld(cfg.seed || String(Date.now()), cfg.w, cfg.h,
371	      (label, p) => loadState.setProgress(label, p),
372	      {
373	        name: cfg.name,
374	        evil: cfg.evil,
375	        onWorldPartial: (world) => loadState.attachWorld(world), // 主线程 fallback：列扫描预览
376	        onPreview: (f) => loadState.attachPreview(f),            // worker 路径：位图预览
377	      });
378	    // onWorldReady 已进游戏；登记世界槽位并保存初始数据
379	    const meta = await worldStore.register({
380	      name: cfg.name, seed: cfg.seed, w: cfg.w, h: cfg.h,
381	      difficulty: cfg.difficulty, evil: cfg.evil,
382	    });
383	    currentWorld = meta;
384	    doSave();
385	  }
386	
387	  /** 设置面板（主菜单含数据栏；游戏内不显示数据栏） */
388	  function openSettings(inGame: boolean) {
389	    new SettingsPanel(root, {
390	      ...(inGame ? {} : {
391	        onQuickLoad: () => void quickLoad(),
392	        onLoadFile: () => {
393	          fileInput.onchange = () => {
394	            const f = fileInput.files?.[0];
395	            if (f) void f.text().then(loadFromJson);
396	            fileInput.value = '';
397	          };
398	          fileInput.click();
399	        },
400	        onImportWld: () => {
401	          wldInput.onchange = () => {
402	            const f = wldInput.files?.[0];
403	            if (f) void f.arrayBuffer().then((ab) => importWld(new Uint8Array(ab)));
404	            wldInput.value = '';
405	          };
406	          wldInput.click();
407	        },
408	      }),
409	      onBack: () => { /* 面板自毁 */ },
410	    });
411	  }
412	
413	  function showTitle() {
414	    audio.play('title');
415	    ui.closeAll();
416	    if (!menuBg) {
417	      menuBg = new MenuBackground(root);
418	      (window as unknown as { __swMenuBg?: MenuBackground }).__swMenuBg = menuBg; // 调试句柄
419	    }
420	    titleMenu?.destroy();
421	    titleMenu = new TitleMenu(root, {
422	      onSinglePlayer: () => showCharacterSelect(),
423	      onSettings: () => openSettings(false),
424	      onQuit: () => ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.QuitUnsupported')),
425	      onCredits: () => ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CreditsLine')),
426	      onMultiplayer: () => showMultiplayerSelect(),
427	    });
428	    installLegacyShim();
429	  }
430	
431	  /** 旧 puppeteer 脚本兼容垫片：select(尺寸)+button(创建) 隐藏 DOM（M7 移除）。
432	   *  挂 root 末尾（在天空画布之上）；标题页按钮在屏幕中央不与垫片（左上角）重叠，
433	   *  querySelector('button') 仍命中垫片——旧探针脚本零修改。 */
434	  function installLegacyShim() {
435	    if (legacyShim) return;
436	    const div = document.createElement('div');
437	    div.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:2px;height:2px;opacity:0.01;z-index:1;';
438	    const sel = document.createElement('select');
439	    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="4200x1200">小</option><option value="6400x1800" selected>中</option><option value="8400x2400">大</option>';
440	    const btn = document.createElement('button');
441	    btn.textContent = '创建新世界';
442	    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
443	      const [w, h] = (sel.value || '6400x1800').split('x').map(Number);
444	      void newWorld('', w, h);
445	    });
446	    div.append(sel, btn);
447	    root.appendChild(div);
448	    legacyShim = div;
449	  }
450	
451	  function quitToMenu() {
452	    game?.destroy();
453	    game = null;
454	    currentWorld = null;
455	    ui.closeAll();
456	    showTitle();
457	  }
458	
459	  function doSave() {
460	    if (!game) return;
461	    // 存档序列化走后台 worker(RLE+base64+JSON 全套离主线程;主线程只付一次
462	    // 全图数组结构化克隆的 memcpy),不可用时 SaveClient 内部回退同步 saveGame
463	    saveClient.ensure();
464	    void saveClient.save(game.world, game.player, Date.now() - playStart).then((json) => {
465	      const mb = json.length / 1024 / 1024;
466	      void kvSetCompat(json).then((where) => {
467	        if (where === 'fail') ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
468	        else {
469	          quickSaveExists = true; // 同会话存档后 quickLoad 立即可用（此前闭包标志只在建流时查一次）
470	          ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.Saved', where === 'idb' ? mb.toFixed(1) + ' MB → IndexedDB' : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Save.Local')));
471	        }
472	      }).catch((e) => {
473	        console.error('kvSet failed', e);
474	        ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
475	      });
476	      // 多世界槽位（M3）：登记到 WorldStore
477	      if (currentWorld) {
478	        void worldStore.save(currentWorld, json, Date.now() - playStart).catch((e) => console.error('worldStore.save failed', e));
479	      }
480	    }).catch((e) => {
481	      // 回退同步 saveGame 也失败(异常世界状态)——如实报告,不留 unhandled rejection
482	      console.error('save failed', e);
483	      ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
484	    });
485	  }
486	
487	  // KvStore 延迟引入避免顶层副作用顺序问题
488	  async function kvSetCompat(json: string): Promise<string> {
489	    const { kvSet } = await import('./save/KvStore');
490	    return kvSet(QUICK_SAVE_KEY, json);
491	  }
492	
493	  // 快速存档存在性预查
494	  void kvHas(QUICK_SAVE_KEY).then((v) => { quickSaveExists = v; });
495	
496	  // 调试桥（puppeteer 脚本用）
497	  (window as unknown as { __swFlow?: unknown }).__swFlow = {
498	    newWorld: (seed: string, w: number, h: number) => newWorld(seed, w, h),
499	    quickLoad,
500	    importWld,
501	    quitToMenu,
502	    doSave,
503	    backToTitle: showTitle,
504	    devMode: () => devMode,
505	    joinGame: (ip: string) => joinGame(ip), // 探针/联机调试桥
506	  };
507	
508	  return {
509	    showTitle,
510	    newWorld,
511	    quickLoad,
512	    importWld,
513	    quitToMenu,
514	    doSave,
515	    openSettings,
516	    get game() { return game; },
517	    playStart: 0,
518	    get playStartNow() { return Date.now() - playStart; },
519	  } as FlowHandle & { playStartNow: number };
520	}
521	

```

16	  const s = atob(b64);

22	function readVarint(u8: Uint8Array, pos: { i: number }): number {
23	  let v = 0, shift = 0;
24	  while (pos.i < u8.length) {
25	    const b = u8[pos.i++];
26	    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
27	    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
28	    shift += 7;
29	  }
30	  return v;
31	}

If the connection fails → unhandled rejection (console error). Also joinGame ignores progress callback (`() => {}`) — no UI feedback. And it doesn't apply selectedAppearance (appearance null at Hello → sends '玩家'). Quality issue. Also note joinGame doesn't pass onProgress → no progress UI → user sees nothing. If user: 多人联机 → 返回 (onBack → showTitle → ui.closeAll() — does closeAll remove the panel? Also check Game.newWorld vs loadWorld onWorldReady → enterGame: in enterGame, titleMenu destroyed. OK.

Minor UX. Also no wss support. Also SSRF-ish: user can enter any URL? It forces ws:// prefix — a user could enter `evil.com/x` → connects. Client-side only, not a server vuln.

Also: MultiplayerSelect input: `input.addEventListener('keydown', Enter → joinBtn.click())` — after joining, panel may remain mounted; Enter key would re-trigger join → duplicate join! If panel not removed. During connecting, pressing Enter again → second joinNetGame → two Games. Verify whether panel is removed on join (ui.closeAll at start of onJoin). If closeAll removes it, fine.

Now check UI.closeAll + TitleMenu. Also check Game constructor cb and whether `newText` exists (onChat → this.newText). And check `Player` import in Game for proxies. Also check `Game.destroy()` clears net? Let me check destroy. If quitToMenu → game.destroy() — does it disconnect net? If not, ws stays open and game destroyed → net.tick never called; netReporter still referenced by destroyed world. Memory leak + ghost player on server. Check destroy().

Let me also check `World` constructor signature `new WorldCtor(w, h, seed, name)` and `clock.tick`.

And check `world.flags` on server: worldDataFrame iterates Object.keys(world.flags) — flags from loaded save could be large? Fine.

Also server: `stripsAround(cx, cy)` uses client-supplied x,y (SpawnTileData) — Math.floor(cx/200) with negative cx → sx negative → x0 negative → filtered. With huge cx (i32 max) → sx*200 overflow? x0 = (sx+dx)*200 — Number precision fine; filtered by x0 < st.w. OK.

Also server: SpawnTileData while state already 10 → re-requests strips (sentStrips dedups) + re-broadcast PlayerActive + re-sends PlayerSpawn + re-syncs all players' appearance. And — importantly — **does NOT reset state**, fine. But each request triggers N broadcasts — amplification. Also the requested area could be anywhere in the world: a client can walk the whole map... no wait, client never sends SpawnTileData again after initial. But a malicious client could request strips for arbitrary coordinates to download the entire world (25 strips per request, dedup per client). That's by design (world data is public to players).

Now check server top-level: `const PORT = parseInt(arg('port','7777')!)` — if user passes `--port abc` → NaN → WebSocketServer({port: NaN}) → listens on random? ws with NaN port → error. Minor.

`MAX_PLAYERS = 255` but LAN advertises max 255.

HTTP LAN server: `http.createServer(...).listen(PORT+1)` — error handling absent; if PORT+1 in use → crash.

CORS '*' on LAN info — exposes server name/online count to any website visited by a LAN user (info leak, minor). Any web page can fetch http://LAN-IP:7778/lan → knows a game server exists. Minor security note.

The UDP broadcast: `lan.send(payload, 8888, broadcastAddr)` — hardcoded broadcast address derived from first non-internal IPv4 — on multi-homed hosts picks wrong NIC. Minor. Also `lan.bind(() => ...)` binds ephemeral port — fine. But no 'error' handler → crash risk.

Also LAN broadcast every 1s with JSON — fine.

Now server: does it broadcast TileBatch back to the originator? broadcast(frame, except=c) — excludes originator. Good (client applied locally already).

But: server applies ops via setTileSilent etc. — silent setters don't trigger listeners; server has no listeners. Fine. However `applyTileOps` SetTile uses setTileSilent — does not clear half/slope... it does (setTileSilent clears half/slope when type===0). OK. But SetHalfBrick/SetSlope write arrays directly — `st.half[st.idx] = o.v ? 1 : 0` — o.v is u16; v=2 → 1. OK. SetSlope: `st.slope[st.idx] = o.v` — v up to 65535 into Uint8Array → truncated to 0-255 → slope out of 0-4 range → could break renderer/physics on other clients (client applyRemote calls st.setSlope which accepts any number → slope=255 stored). Slope validation missing → corrupted world state from malicious client propagates to everyone. SECURITY (integrity).

SetWire: o.v up to 65535 → server `st.setWireSilent` → setWire → `bits & 63` masked. OK masked. Client applyRemote st.setWire masks too. OK.

SetActuated: `!!o.v` fine.

SetWall: v up to 65535 into Uint16Array — wall id unvalidated (max 366) → client TILE_DEFS/WallTiler may index undefined → render issues. Minor integrity.

SetTile: v up to 65535 type unvalidated (max ~500) → TILE_DEFS[v] undefined → isSolid returns false; renderer may break. Integrity — a malicious client can place invalid tile types on the server world, persisted to save! Security (world corruption, saved).

Also negative fx/fy? u16 so 0-65535.

Now the frame length semantics: len includes the 3-byte header (server writes len = this.len where len counts from 3). Client FrameParser: payload = buf.subarray(p+3, p+len). Consistent. Good.

FrameParser.drain returns bool — callers ignore. Fine.

Another protocol bug: NetWriter.finish() `if (this.len - 3 > 65535)` — checks payload > 65535 but len field is u16 so total len must be ≤65535; payload = len-3 ≤ 65532. The check allows payload up to 65535 → total len up to 65538 → dv.setUint16(0, 65538) truncates to 2! Off-by-3: a payload of exactly 65533..65535 passes the check but the written len wraps → receiver sees len=2 (<3) → dirty stream → permanent desync. Edge case: encodeStrip max is 64007 so not hit today, but the guard is wrong. Verify: `this.len - 3 > 65535` should be `this.len > 65535`. Yes — off-by-3 bug. RELIABILITY (latent).

NetReader.str(): u16 max 65535 bytes — decode allocates; server handle stores appearance/name uncapped → a client can send 64KB names repeatedly, each broadcast to all clients → amplification ×N players ×64KB. Also c.name replaced each Hello. DoS amplification. Also — `r.str()` on server for chat capped at 200 chars AFTER decode, but the raw 64KB still decoded. Fine-ish. Flag as security (no length caps on name/appearance).

Bigger: server has no cap on message rate → a client can flood PlayerState at any rate → broadcast amplification to N clients. Rate limiting absent. SECURITY/PERF.

  async setLanguage(culture: LangCultureInfo | string | number): Promise<boolean> {
    const name = typeof culture === 'object' ? culture.name : normalizeLang(culture);
    if (this.activeCulture?.name === name && this.loaded) return true;

For the server, more precisely: any throw inside `ws.on('message')` handler → since it's a sync listener invocation from the socket 'data' event → uncaughtException → default handler prints and EXITS. So yes, whole-server crash from one malformed packet. That's the top crash finding. Which messages can throw with a well-framed (len matches bytes) but truncated payload? Any message where the handler reads more fields than present: TileBatch count > actual ops (RangeError), SyncPlayer str u16 read at end (RangeError), Hello ver u16 missing (RangeError), Ping f64 missing (RangeError), NetModules module u16 missing. All reachable by a malicious client with zero auth (pre-handshake even: Hello/Ping/SyncPlayer/NetModules are not gated!). E.g., connect, send frame [len=4][msgId=154] with 1-byte payload → r.f64() → RangeError → server crash. Trivially exploitable. CRITICAL.

Let me be precise about frame construction: [u16 len][u8 msgId][payload]. len=4 → total 4 bytes → payload 1 byte. Ping reads f64 → NetReader.dv.getFloat32... f64 needs 8 bytes from p=0 within byteLength=1 → RangeError. Yes.

I'm confident. Now also confirm server `ws.on('message')` has no try/catch — lines 293-296. Yes.

Also confirm there's no global `process.on('uncaughtException')` — grep. Let me check.

# SandboxWorld 多人联机 v1 审计报告

## 【崩溃级】

**C1. 服务器可被单个畸形包整进程打崩(最高危)**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:293-296`(无 try/catch)+ `game/src/net/protocol.ts:87-108`(NetReader 零越界检查)+ `index.ts` 全文仅 1 处 try(saveWorld),无 `process.on('uncaughtException')`。
- 场景:NetReader 的 `u16/u32/i32/f16/f32/f64` 全部走 `DataView.get*`,越过 payload 尾部直接抛 `RangeError`。构造一个帧 `[u16 len=4][msgId=154(Ping)][1 字节]` 发给服务器,`index.ts:271 r.f64()` 立即 RangeError。该异常在 `ws.on('message')` 同步监听器里抛出 → uncaughtException → **整个服务器进程退出,所有玩家掉线**。无需握手即可触发(Ping/Hello/SyncPlayer/NetModules 均无状态门禁)。TileBatch 的 count 字段虚报(`readTileBatch` 循环 n 次而 payload 不足)同理。
- 修法:`handle()` 外层包 try/catch(出错即 `ws.close(1002)`),或给 NetReader 加 `remaining()` 检查并在 `drain` 回调统一捕获;同时补 `process.on('uncaughtException')` 兜底日志。

**C2. decodeStrip 的 RLE count=0 → 客户端浏览器标签页死循环冻结**
- 位置:`game/src/net/protocol.ts:246-257`,经 `ClientNet.ts:125` 触发。
- 场景:`while (written < w*h) { const count = r.u16(); ... for (k<count && written<w*h) ... }`——count=0 时内层循环不执行、written 不增长,**外层 while 永不退出**。恶意/出错的服务器(或中间链路损坏)发一条 count=0 的 strip,浏览器主线程死循环,页面永久冻结,只能杀标签页。同理 payload 截断时 `r.u16()` 抛 RangeError,store 被写了一半(部分应用)。
- 修法:`if (count === 0) break/return null`,并对 reader 剩余字节做前置校验(`count*1 与 remaining` 估算)。

**C3. 服务器自动存档 100% 失败(静默)**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:61` + `game/src/save/SaveFile.ts:49-54`。
- 场景:`saveGame(world, { hp:100, x, y, inventory:[], selected:0 } as never, 0)`——saveGame 构造 playerData 时读 `player.inv.slots`(`SaveFile.ts:51`),而 stub 没有 `inv` 字段 → `undefined.slots` TypeError,在序列化开始前就抛出。被 `saveWorld` 的 catch 吞掉,仅打印 `[save] 失败`。**所有 tile 修改永不持久化**,SIGINT/SIGTERM 存档同样失败(`index.ts:71-72`),进程退出后世界回档。
- 修法:stub 补 `inv: { slots: [], selected: 0 }` 等完整形状(或给 saveGame 加仅世界的重载),并在启动时做一次试存验证。

**C4. `npm run build` 完全不可用(已实测 210 个错误)**
- 位置:`server/tsconfig.json`(`include: ["src/**/*.ts"]`、`lib: ["ES2022"]` 无 DOM)+ `server/src/index.ts:15-19`(.ts 扩展名 import)、`index.ts:31`(TS2345)。
- 实测:`./node_modules/.bin/tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit` 输出 210 个错误:TS5097×5(.ts 扩展名)、TS2345(SAVE_INTERVAL 的 `string|undefined`)、以及因 import 链拉入整个 `game/src`(WorldGen→Door、settle→LiquidSim、serialize→data/items 等)后大量 TS2304/TS2584(HTMLCanvasElement/document/window)。**服务器的类型安全保障为零**,任何协议改动错误只能等运行时爆炸。
- 修法:加 `allowImportingTsExtensions`;`arg()` 返回收紧;给 server 单独建一个隔离的 `game-core` 入口(或用 `paths` + 独立 tsconfig 只覆盖 net/world/save),不要让 tsc 拉入渲染层。

**C5. FrameParser 脏流不丢弃 → 永久失步 + 缓冲无限增长**
- 位置:`game/src/net/protocol.ts:137` 与 `:144`。
- 场景:`len < 3 || len > 65535` 时 `break` 但 **p 不前进**,随后 `if (p < buf.length) this.chunks.push(buf.subarray(p))` 把脏字节原样放回。注释写"脏流:丢弃",实际一字节都没丢。下一次 drain 读到同一个坏 len 再次 break——连接永久卡死,且后续到达的所有数据持续追加进 chunks(`total` 无上限)。配合"ws 消息无 maxPayload"(见 S1),这是内存膨胀路径。
- 修法:脏流时 `p += 1`(按字节重同步)或直接断开连接;同时给 `total` 设上限(如 256KB)超限即踢。

## 【安全级】

**S1. ws 无 maxPayload + 广播无背压 → 慢客户端/恶意客户端 OOM**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:288`(`new WebSocketServer({ port: PORT })`,默认 maxPayload 100 MiB)、`index.ts:98-100`(send 无 bufferedAmount 检查)、`index.ts:102-107`(broadcast)。
- 场景 A:单客户端发一个 100MB ws 消息,`parser.append`(`protocol.ts:119` `new Uint8Array(data)`)再复制一份 → 200MB/连接。场景 B:15Hz PlayerState + tile 广播对每个慢客户端无限缓冲(`ws.send` 的 bufferedAmount 无人查看),255 个慢客户端可耗尽服务器内存。场景 C:TileBatch 触发的广播把 1 个包放大到 N 个在线玩家。
- 修法:`new WebSocketServer({ port, maxPayload: 65535 + 16 })`;send 前 `if (c.ws.bufferedAmount > 4<<20) { 踢出或暂停 }`。

**S2. 未握手消息缺状态门禁:SyncPlayer / 聊天 / Ping / SpawnTileData**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:228-233`(SyncPlayer)、`:254-268`(NetModules)、`:270-274`(Ping)、`:211-227`(SpawnTileData 仅查 `slot<0`)。
- 场景:连接后不发 Hello 直接发 SyncPlayer → `c.slot === -1`,广播 `u8(-1)` = **slot 255**(与 `MAX_PLAYERS=255` 边界值撞车,`:76`),所有客户端凭空出现"玩家255";外观字符串长度无上限(u16 最大 64KB),每次广播放大到全部在线玩家,且被后续每个新加入者再次拉取。聊天同理:未握手客户端以 slot 255 冒名发言(`:262`)。Ping 则是 C1 的崩服入口。
- 修法:所有 case 开头统一 `if (c.state < 1) return;`;SyncPlayer 改为服务器权威 slot;限制 name/appearance 字节数(如 512B)。

**S3. Hello 可重发 → slot 泄漏 → 服务器被占满**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:188-204`。
- 场景:已握手(state=1/10)的客户端再发一次 Hello:`allocSlot()` 分配新 slot 覆盖 `c.slot`,旧 slot 的 `slotUsed` 永不释放(断线时 `:300` 只释放最后一个)。重复 255 次后 `allocSlot()` 返回 -1,正常玩家全部被踢"服务器已满"。同时 `c.state` 被打回 1,该玩家静默退出广播圈。
- 修法:`case Msg.Hello: if (c.state >= 1) { kick('重复握手'); return; }`。

**S4. 看门狗可被垃圾帧永久续命;无握手超时**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:186`(`c.lastSeen = 0` 对任意消息生效,含 unknown msgId)与 `:309-317`。
- 场景:客户端每秒发一个 `[len=3][msgId=255]` 的合法帧,default 分支丢弃但 lastSeen 清零 → 永不超时。255 个这种连接(不握手、不占 slot)不会触发"服务器已满",但会占满文件描述符与 clients Set。
- 修法:只对握手后消息重置 lastSeen;对 `state===0` 的连接加独立短超时(如 10s)。

**S5. tile 操作值域零校验 → 世界数据损坏并入库**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:169-181`。
- 场景:`SetSlope` 的 `o.v` 是 u16 直写 `st.slope[idx]`(可到 255,合法域 0-4);`SetTile` 的 v 可到 65535(TILE_DEFS 表外 id);`SetWall` 同理(合法 0-366)。恶意客户端把脏值写入服务器权威世界,**随后被(修复后的)saveWorld 持久化**,并经 `:251` 广播污染所有客户端(渲染/碰撞查表越界行为未定义)。
- 修法:`applyTileOps` 里对 v 做域校验(type ≤ TILE_DEFS.length、wall ≤ 366、slope ≤ 4),非法 op 整包丢弃并计数踢人。

**S6. 无任何速率限制**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:234-252`。
- 场景:PlayerState/TileBatch 收到即广播,客户端可无限制灌包,服务器按 (1+在线数) 放大转发。一条 TileBatch 帧内最多约 5957 个 op,每次触发约 6k 次静默写 + 重编码 + N 份广播。
- 修法:每客户端每秒消息数/op 数 token bucket。

**S7.(低)LAN 发现 HTTP 端点 CORS `*` + 无鉴权信息暴露**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:348-357`。任意网页可探测同网段 `:7778/lan`,获取服务器名/在线数/世界尺寸。局域网 v1 可接受,公网部署前必须收紧。另:该 http server 与 `lan` dgram socket 均无 `'error'` 处理(`:322`、`:348`),端口占用即触发未处理 error 事件崩溃。

## 【可靠性级】

**R1. SyncPlayer C→S/S→C 字段不对称 → 远程玩家外观全坏**
- 位置:`game/src/net/ClientNet.ts:110`(发送 `.u8(mySlot).str(app)`)vs `server/src/index.ts:230`(只 `r.str()`,不读前置 u8)。
- 场景:服务器把 slot 字节当成 u16 长度的低字节读,长度错位 → appearance 解码为乱码 → 原样广播给所有人 → 各客户端 `JSON.parse` 失败(`Game.ts:4712` catch 吞掉)→ **远程玩家永远渲染默认外观**。还会把长达数千字符的乱码串反复广播(放大)。
- 修法:服务器侧先 `r.u8()` 再 `r.str()`(对齐 PlayerState 的覆写模式),或客户端去掉 u8。

**R2. 角色名/外观根本没上传过**
- 位置:`game/src/ClientNet.ts:66-72`(onopen 读 `this.game.player`)+ `Game.ts:139`(`player!: Player` 定赋值)+ `Game.ts:533`(loadWorld 才创建)。
- 场景:连接时 `Game.player` 是 undefined → Hello 名字恒为 `'玩家'`、SyncPlayer 外观恒为 `'{}'`;而 SyncPlayer **只在 PlayerSlot 时发一次**(`ClientNet.ts:110`),loadWorld 创建 Player、`applyAppearance` 生效后从不重发 → 联机中其他人永远看到"玩家"+默认外观。与 R1 叠加后选人系统在联机模式下完全失效。
- 修法:把所选 Appearance 在 `joinNetGame` 前注入(或 connect 前 new 占位 Player),并在 onWorldReady/loadWorld 完成后重发一次 SyncPlayer。

**R3. 没有 section 流式续传 → 走出出生点 ±400 格就是空气**
- 位置:`game/src/ClientNet.ts:244` 是全代码库唯一一处 `SpawnTileData` 发送点(已 grep 验证);`ClientNet.ts:123-124` 收到晚到的 TileSection 直接 `if (!this.pendingWorld) return`。
- 场景:初始只下发出生点周围 5×5 条带(200×20,即横向 ±2 条带 = ±400 格)。玩家向任一方向走 400 格后,store 全 0(空气)→ 掉出世界/穿图。v1 的"section 兴趣管理"只有一次性下载,没有移动续传。这是联机模式最大的功能性缺口。
- 修法:Game 侧在玩家跨条带边界时调用 `net.requestSection(cx, cy)`;ClientNet 恢复 `pendingWorld==null` 时把 strip 写入 `gameWorld.store` 并整块标脏(decodeStrip 的注释 `protocol.ts:237` 明确要求调用方标脏,当前无人做——现在没炸只是因为永远不会收到第二批 strip)。

**R4. tileQueue 256 上限静默丢弃 → 不可恢复的静默分叉**
- 位置:`game/src/net/ClientNet.ts:253`(`if (this.tileQueue.length >= 256) return;`)。
- 场景:挖掘会经由 TileStore 钩子逐格上报,且无同坐标 op 合并;爆炸/砍树/落沙级联(沙队列 `Game.ts:526-531` 每步 setTile)在单 tick 内可轻松超 256 → 溢出的 op 被无声丢弃,本地世界与服务器/其他玩家从此分叉,无任何告警与对账。
- 修法:超限时至少 toast/console 警告;按 `(x,y,a)` 去重合并(后写覆盖前写);或满时合并为更大批次而非丢包。

**R5. applyRemote 的 netSuppress 不异常安全**
- 位置:`game/src/net/ClientNet.ts:298-310`。
- 场景:`st.netSuppress = true` 后逐 op 写入,`st.setTile` 等若中途抛出(数组越界以外的任何意外),`:310` 的复位不会执行 → suppress 永久为 true → 之后所有本地挖掘不再上报,静默分叉。修法:try/finally。

**R6. drain() 先清缓冲再解析 → 一个坏帧拖死同批全部后续帧**
- 位置:`game/src/net/protocol.ts:130-131`(解析前清空 chunks/total)。
- 场景:合并缓冲在回调抛异常时已被销毁,同一 TCP 段里的后续帧与半帧全部丢失(客户端侧;服务器侧因 C1 直接崩)。客户端表现为莫名不同步。修法:解析成功后再截断缓冲,或异常时回滚。

**R7. Game.destroy 不断开网络 → 幽灵玩家 + 世界内存泄漏**
- 位置:`game/src/core/Game.ts:744-755`(destroy 无 `this.net?.disconnect()`)。
- 场景:联机中"返回主菜单"(`mainFlow.ts:451-457`)→ ws 保持连接 → 服务器上该玩家永不下线(直到 120s 看门狗),其他玩家看到一个不动的幽灵;同时 `ClientNet.gameWorld` 仍持有整个 World(6400×1800 = 11.5M 格 × 10 个 TypedArray,数百 MB)无法回收。反复进出会累积。
- 修法:destroy() 第一行 `this.net?.disconnect(); this.net = null; this.remotePlayerProxies = [];`。

**R8. 游戏内断线没有任何流程处理**
- 位置:`game/src/net/ClientNet.ts:77-82`(onclose→onKick)+ `Game.ts:4671-4674`(onKick 只 toast+reject,已 resolve 的 promise reject 为 no-op)。
- 场景:联机中途服务器重启/断网 → toast 一条"与服务器断开连接",**游戏循环继续跑**,玩家在无主世界里继续单人游玩,net.tick 因 active=false 不再发送,远端玩家代理永远留在场上(`syncRemotePlayerProxies` 每 15 帧照跑),无重连、无回菜单。
- 修法:onKick 时若已在游戏中,应暂停并弹"断线重连/返回菜单"。

**R9. 暂停/切后台超过 120 秒必被踢**
- 位置:`game/src/net/ClientNet.ts:276-291`(唯一心跳是 sendPlayerState)+ `Game.ts:772`(paused 时 fixedUpdate 跳过 → postUpdate 不跑 → tick 不发)。
- 场景:打开暂停菜单或切走标签页 2 分钟以上(后台 rAF 也停),服务器看门狗(`index.ts:309-317`)踢人。协议里专门定义的 `Msg.Ping`(`protocol.ts:27`)客户端从未发送(`ClientNet.ts:212-213` 只接收忽略)——独立心跳是死代码。
- 修法:加一个与游戏循环解耦的 30s Ping 定时器。

**R10. joinNetGame 超时兜底的多处时序问题**
- 位置:`game/src/core/Game.ts:4677-4683`。
- 具体点:(a) 注释写"10s 未完成握手"、代码是 30s;(b) 成功后定时器不清除(30s 后空跑一次,因 gameWorld 已设而无害,但属泄漏);(c) reject 后二次 reject 为 no-op,`net.disconnect()` 二次调用无害——这两点验证为**非致命**;(d) 真正的问题:`this.net = net`(`:4664`)先于 `loadWorld` 执行,若 loadWorld 失败(资源加载异常)走 reject,`this.net` 仍指向活跃连接且无人清理,ws 挂在废弃 Game 上。
- 修法:`clearTimeout`;reject 分支统一 `net.disconnect(); this.net = null`。

**R11. 远端玩家代理共享本地背包 → 渲染串味**
- 位置:`game/src/core/Game.ts:4704`(`new Player(rp.x, rp.y, this.player.inv)`)+ `game/src/render/Renderer.ts:462-464` 及 drawPlayer 内 `p.inv.heldItem()`、`dollEquipFromInv(p.inv, ...)`。
- 场景:drawPlayer 从 inv 取手持物与盔甲纸娃娃 → **每个远程玩家都画着本地玩家当前的手持物和盔甲**;网络字段 `selectedItem`(`ClientNet.ts:179`)被解码后从未用于渲染。属于只读共享,不会崩,但视觉上全员"克隆人"。
- 修法:给代理一个独立空 `Inventory`,并按 `selectedItem` + SyncPlayer 中的装备数据填充;至少传 `new Inventory()`。

**R12. IP 输入含端口/非法字符 → Promise 永不 settle,进度条卡死**
- 位置:`game/src/mainFlow.ts:298`(`ws://${ip}:7777`)+ `game/src/ui/MultiplayerSelect.ts:33`(placeholder 明示可输"域名",用户输 `host:8080` 很自然)+ `ClientNet.ts:63`。
- 场景:`new WebSocket("ws://host:8080:7777")` 对非法 URL **同步抛 SyntaxError**,发生在 `joinNetGame` 的 Promise executor 内 → 同步异常直接穿透 `onJoin` 点击处理器:已 showProgress 的进度条永不消失,无 toast、不回主菜单,30s 兜底也不会触发。
- 修法:connect 用 try/catch 并 reject;输入侧允许 `host:port` 形式解析。

**R13. MultiplayerSelect 面板永不移除 → 重复堆叠、进游戏后挡输入**
- 位置:`game/src/mainFlow.ts:313`(`root.appendChild(panel.root)`)+ `game/src/ui/UI.ts:121-124,1097-1103`(closeAll 清的是 `ui.root` 即 `sw-root`,不是 flow 的 `#game-root`)。
- 场景:验证过 DOM 结构——`#game-root` 下有 Game 渲染 canvas(`Game.ts:302-304` `renderer.attach(root)`)、`sw-root`、以及 append 的面板。面板没有 destroy(对比 `TitleMenu.ts:136-139`、`CharSelect.ts:176` 的自移除)。后果:(a) 多次"多人联机→返回"面板逐个堆叠;(b) onJoin 里 `ui.closeAll()` 清不掉它,连接期间和**成功进游戏后**面板仍然挂在上层、可点击(默认 pointer-events:auto),挡住游戏输入,Enter 键(`MultiplayerSelect.ts:42-44`)还能再次触发加入 → 双 Game 双 rAF 循环;(c) 失败路径 `showTitle()` 后面板与标题菜单同屏。
- 修法:给 MultiplayerSelect 加 destroy/onBack 自移除;onJoin 首行 `panel.root.remove()` 并禁用按钮。

## 【性能级】

**P1. saveWorld 全程同步阻塞事件循环**
- 位置:`server/src/index.ts:57-70`。`saveGame`(RLE+base64)+`writeFileSync`+`renameSync` 全同步;6400×1800 世界序列化是秒级 CPU,期间所有 ws 收发停摆,玩家集体卡顿。修法:挪到 worker/子进程,或至少分片异步写。

**P2. FrameParser 双重复制 + 每帧新建 DataView**
- 位置:`game/src/net/protocol.ts:117-121`(append 再复制一次 `new Uint8Array(data)`)、`:127-129`(drain 又合并成新数组)、`:135`(while 循环内每帧 `new DataView`)。15Hz×N 玩家的 PlayerState 小包路径上每包 3 次分配。修法:Buffer 复用/直接在原 buffer 上建一次 DataView;仅当 chunks.length>1 时才合并。

**P3. 广播无背压(同 S1 场景 B),发送路径不检查 readyState 之外的任何东西** —— `server/src/index.ts:98-100`。

**P4. 单机模式 netReporter 开销:验证为可忽略(通过)**
- `game/src/world/TileStore.ts:118,127,136,155,170,184` 均为 `if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress)` 单分支,op 对象字面量仅在 reporter 存在时分配——单机零分配、两个属性读+一次分支,无问题。

**P5. encodeStrip 最坏情况逼近包上限(仅 1.5KB 余量)**
- 位置:`game/src/net/protocol.ts:195`(注释称"最坏 57KB")与 `:217-233`(实际 4000 格 × 16B = 64000B + 头 ≈ 64007B)。全异构 strip 距 65535 只剩 ~1528B;任何调大 STRIP_W/H 的改动都会让 `finish()` 抛异常,而它跑在 SpawnTileData 消息处理器里 → 触发 C1 崩服。修法:按 rect 面积预算,超限自动降级为分条发送。

## 【代码质量】

**Q1. `setWireSilent` 直接别名 `setWire`,silent 语义名存实亡**
- 位置:`game/src/world/TileStore.ts:173-175`。调用 setWireSilent 会走 netReporter 上报。当前这在客户端恰好是**需要的行为**(Wiring 走 silent 路径但导线需要同步),在服务器侧因 reporter 为 null 而无害——但语义与命名相反,下一个以为 "Silent=不上报" 的调用方会制造双报。建议拆成 `setWireRaw`(纯写)与显式上报决策,或改名并加注释。

**Q2. NetWriter.grow()/finish() 的 DataView 一致性:验证为正确(通过)**
- `protocol.ts:50-58`:grow 后 `this.buf` 与 `this.dv` 同步重建,所有写方法先 grow 再访问,无失效引用。`finish()`:81 返回 `buf.subarray` 活视图,已核查全部调用点(均一次性 new NetWriter 后立即 finish),无复用改写风险——但建议在类注释里写明"finish 后禁止再写",防后人踩坑。

**Q3. finish() 长度守卫差 3(潜伏)**
- `protocol.ts:78`:`if (this.len - 3 > 65535)` 应为 `this.len > 65535`。payload 在 65533..65535 之间时通过检查,但 `setUint16(0, len)` 溢出回绕成小值 → 接收端读到 len<3 → 触发 C5 脏流失步。当前调用点最大 64007 未踩中,属地雷。

**Q4. readTileBatch/writeTileBatch 对称性:验证为对称(通过)**
- `protocol.ts:167-190`:字段顺序、SetTile 的条件 fx/fy、`v & 0xffff` 两端一致。唯一隐患是 read 侧不校验 count 与 payload 匹配(见 C1)。

**Q5. 其余小项**
- `ClientNet.ts:128`:进度恒为 0.5(`onProgress('接收世界数据', 0.5)`),进度条纹丝不动直到"完成";应按 `1 - pendingStrips/total` 推进。
- `index.ts:109` 注释"5×3 section 语义"与代码 5×5=25 条带不符(`:117-118`)。
- `index.ts:61` `as never` 压制类型错误直接导致 C3;应定义真实的 SavePlayerView。
- `mainFlow.ts:289-292` `joinGame` 调试桥:无进度回调(`() => {}`)、失败产生 unhandled rejection、也不应用所选角色。
- `index.ts:323-334` LAN 广播地址取"第一个非内网 IPv4",多网卡主机(有线+Wi-Fi/VPN)会选错网段;建议对每个 NIC 各发一份或做成参数。
- `server/src/index.ts:31` TS2345(`arg()` 返回 `string|undefined` 传 parseInt),`arg()` 签名应按 def 是否给出做重载。

## 验证结论摘要(任务清单中的"检查项"判定)

| 检查项 | 结论 |
|---|---|
| NetWriter.grow 的 dv 一致性 | 正确(buf/dv 同步换) |
| finish() subarray 使用时机 | 当前安全(无 writer 复用),加注释防回归 |
| FrameParser drain 半包/脏流 | 半包正确;脏流是 **C5 级 bug** |
| encodeStrip/decodeStrip 越界 | decode 越界检查正确;**count=0 死循环(C2)** |
| read/writeTileBatch 对称性 | 对称;read 无 count 校验(C1 载体) |
| Node 兼容(btoa/atob/DOM) | 运行时可用(Node18+ 全局 btoa/atob/performance/TextEncoder,`typeof window` 有守卫);**类型构建崩溃(C4)** |
| 内存泄漏 | clients/slotUsed 基本干净;**Hello 重发漏 slot(S3)、destroy 不断网漏 World(R7)** |
| 背压 | 缺失(S1) |
| 状态门禁 | SyncPlayer/聊天/Ping/SpawnTileData 缺失(S2) |
| saveWorld | **100% 失败(C3)** |
| ws error / handle 异常 | **无兜底,崩进程(C1)** |
| 30s 超时/reject 双调 | 双调无害;loadWorld 失败清理缺失(R10d) |
| proxy 共享 inv | **渲染串味(R11)** |
| MultiplayerSelect/TitleMenu 流程 | **面板泄漏 + 进游戏挡输入(R13)** |
| 单机 netReporter 开销 | 可忽略(通过) |

最优先修复顺序建议:**C1 → C2 → S1/S2 → R3 → C3 → R7/R13 → C4**。C1/C2 是可被单个包/单条消息触发的硬崩,S1-S3 决定这服务器能否暴露到局域网之外,R3 决定联机模式是否可玩超过 5 分钟。
```
